These cumulative appendices have accepted component review; whole-transaction acceptance remains separate; they do not advance a canonical highwater by themselves.

This is current WEB reconciliation. SHARED means the pair's represented local speech/actions, not shared private reasons; PRIVATE remains Sendai/reader access; MIXED separates its action and private clauses; OWN_ACTION does not imply Miyagi witnessed hiding while turned away. Earlier reports, current acts, conditional threats and prospective possibilities retain distinct clocks. Grouped actor-event spans may overlap; observations retain the full interleaved order. Novel-only own appraisals, lexical contrasts and ruby stay in the separate finite correspondence.

OwnSendai and directly observed Miyagi advance fromE27 through performed hiding/search, differentiated refusals/interventions and specifically honored change, then reciprocal command closure and seated proximity. OwnMiyagiE24, Sendai-observed-through-MiyagiE24 and all adult/historical LN10clocks remain. The location is finally confirmed, but eraser extraction/return and visit departure are not narrated. ### E0028 retained disclosure boundaries

Private loss/purpose, retaliation, sensory association, counter-affect, endurance/shared-line and plea-waiting accounts stay attributed. They cannot replace actual voiced limits, performed interventions, real particular compliance or the final explicit ending. The unknown cause of naming aversion and final inaccessible face/thought remain positive information limits. Novel-only self-reassurance/fairness/toy appraisals do not enter WEB facts or participant receipts. At WEB204 the emptied glass is placed passively with placer unexpressed/Miyagi contextual; the explicit connected LN419 placement is a separate variant. No lip act, mark, complete prior motive, injury diagnosis, outside disclosure, extraction, second search or visit departure is added.

## E0029 current source appendix — 2026-10-31

This E29 cumulative component reconciles the complete original WEB against the accepted source, material and finite components. Independent cumulative review and root acceptance are complete; whole-transaction and publication checks remain separate, and this component alone advances no canonical coverage. Presentation, depicted event and admission clocks stay distinct; remembered search/naming/neck acts are not newly performed. Private narration, unissued commands, imagined alternatives, future plans and actual message/arrival have different receipt gates. Novel-only differences remain in the reading’s separate comparison.

| Endpoint: PROP-KAK-JP-E0005-017 v2 | PRESERVE | Current wishing she had assigned homework is counterfactual, while the older illustrative examples and E25's actual recalled order retain distinct clocks. No new instruction, current Sendai work or completed older assignment follows. |
| Endpoint: PROP-KAK-JP-E0005-020 v2 | PRESERVE | No-errand initiation to Sendai adds a motive/type of summons, not a new substitute choice, universal exclusivity or change to the earlier bounded intended-candidate/message-only testimony. |
| Endpoint: PROP-KAK-JP-E0005-025 v1 | PRESERVE | Preserve the literal original Miyagi-subject declaration and accepted phone request. Current broader narrated school promise is later application, handled at the current school-practice successor/rule; it does not replace the original speakers or words. |
| Endpoint: PROP-KAK-JP-E0007-014 v1 | PRESERVE | Current self-admitted inability to make karaage is a different private skill statement, captured with the current household/cooking account. It does not change the old handmade-gift claim, visual size/taste appraisal or reveal its making. |
| Endpoint: PROP-KAK-JP-E0008-008 v1 | PRESERVE | The older finger recollection keeps its unspecified earlier identity, pain and mixed affect. ## E0033 current WEB-only cumulative appendix — 2026-11-01

This E33 component carries separate source/typed A4, material A5, finite A4, independent cumulative A3 and complete-reading A4 acceptances. Whole introduction and publication retain their own gates. Novel-only class, seat, names, yesterday and self-explanation do not become WEB facts. Last-year acceptance and earlier eye closure/expulsion retain their reported event clocks; WEB supplies no exact elapsed time from E32. All earlier source, recipient, error, countercase, later novel/supplement and readiness/model histories remain. Raw originals and private evidence stay local.
